- **locale-edge-sweep**: Find internationalisation defects by sweeping every screen through a fixed hostile locale set, not the next market's language. Externalise strings as ICU messages, then sweep: a pseudo-locale with about 40 percent expansion, Turkish dotless i in case mapping, right-to-left with logical properties, German compounds, Thai and Japanese without spaces, collation for sorting, name and address formats, and numbers, currency and dates via the platform formatter; gate it in CI. Use when someone says "we need to support Arabic", "the layout breaks in German", "plurals are wrong in Polish" or "Swedish… ([greenblacked/AI](https://github.com/greenblacked/AI/tree/HEAD/plugins/client-apps/skills/locale-edge-sweep))

- **locale-qa-agent-browser-automation-fai-07f297bcee5280fb**: Covers locale QA agents failing RTL screenshot comparisons: use RTL baselines or mirror normalization. - **locale-qa-agent-compared-screenshots-a-e745618564a4e00a**: Covers locale QA visual checks failing across OS fonts: pin one OS or normalize with webfonts. Use it when macOS vs Linux screenshots differ. Not for same-OS diffs. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/locale-qa-agent-compared-screenshots-a-e745618564a4e00a))
- **locale-qa-agent-crashed-on-xliff-file-1e29278e9560feba**: Covers locale QA agents crashing on huge XLIFF files: stream with a SAX parser. Use it for OOMs on large files. Not for slow small files. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/locale-qa-agent-crashed-on-xliff-file-1e29278e9560feba))
- **locale-qa-agent-failed-to-catch-pseudo-a75b89e887a0a13b**: Covers QA agents mistaking pseudo-locale expansion for bugs: detect pseudo and use expansion-aware assertions. Use it when pseudo runs fail overflow en masse. Not for real locale overflows. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/locale-qa-agent-failed-to-catch-pseudo-a75b89e887a0a13b))
- **locale-qa-agent-flagged-correct-plural-a04de991a30377c7**: Fixes the locale QA agent reporting false missing-plural failures. Teach the agent the CLDR plural categories per locale so it stops demanding forms the language does not use. Use when QA fails on correct plural sets. Not for genuinely missing forms. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/locale-qa-agent-flagged-correct-plural-a04de991a30377c7))
- **locale-qa-agent-loop-detected-re-runni-e6e0a20fdd5240f4**: Covers locale QA agents looping failed screenshot suites: quarantine after one retry and finish the suite. Use it when the same specs fail every run. Not for systemic flakiness. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/locale-qa-agent-loop-detected-re-runni-e6e0a20fdd5240f4))
- **locale-qa-agent-missed-truncated-germa-0a49f1506ab2da8a**: Covers QA agents missing truncated German strings: assert scrollWidth vs clientWidth on fixed elements. Use it when buttons cut off but tests pass. Not for intentional ellipsis. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/locale-qa-agent-missed-truncated-germa-0a49f1506ab2da8a))
- **locale-qa-agent-signed-off-with-missin-4395074d7d039acd**: Covers QA agents signing off with missing plural forms: assert per-locale category completeness before sign-off. Use it when releases fail on absent plural keys. Not for wrong plural copy. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/locale-qa-agent-signed-off-with-missin-4395074d7d039acd))
- **locale-qa-agent-timed-out-scanning-mon-7e12335be87f994d**: Covers locale QA agents timing out on monorepos: shard by package with per-shard timeouts and merge reports. Use it when whole-repo scans never finish. Not for wrong results. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/locale-qa-agent-timed-out-scanning-mon-7e12335be87f994d))
- **locale-qa-linguistic-functional**: Locale-specific QA — linguistic (Xbench, Okapi Checkmate, Vale) and functional (Playwright screenshot diff).
